# Photojournal: all 717 entries reviewed individually

27 September 2026 · cssEarth baseline `60ef02395df5c466027b8a70214b09cbc1afc570`

717 distinct supplied PIA IDs; 716 NASA descriptions retrieved. PIA00451 remains inaccessible and has an explicit unresolved row. This is individual metadata/source-description screening, not native-pixel qualification.

[All proposals](proposals/README.md) · [Structured decisions](evidence/previous-audits.json) · [Slice and export](README.md#slice-the-audit)

## Decisions

| Decision | Entries |
| --- | ---: |
| deferred-scope | 176 |
| candidate | 315 |
| alternative | 130 |
| not-data-input | 25 |
| duplicate-family | 60 |
| access-unresolved | 1 |
| existing-reference | 9 |
| owned-elsewhere | 1 |

Candidate means a specific source lead requiring the stated checks. Alternative means current-family overlap, an older/lower-value option or an interpreted display that does not establish a useful addition. Duplicate-family means a separately inspected variant assigned to the same proposal. Existing-reference denotes an exact repository mention; it does not on its own prove live display. Deferred-scope preserves Mars leads under the user's exclusion. Owned-elsewhere preserves the Enceladus VIMS work already assigned to another chat when checked.
